In the second assumption, the Wasserstein distance between the conditional distributions of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}
given
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}^{\prime}
is assumed to be controlled by the norm of the task
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}
.
This is a slight difference with
2
where we assumed a dependence on
1+\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\rVert
. This is however without loss of generality as we can always consider
\widetilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}=(1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{R}}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d}+1}
and redefine the task distribution accordingly and this cosmetic change simplifies the presentation.
We could also consider a dependence on
\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}^{\prime}\rVert
, see
Theorem

E.1
, but we omit this for simplicity.

Assumption 6

(Finite moments of the task distribution)
.

There exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\geq 2
integer such that
\ex[\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\rVert^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}]<+\infty
.

Our theory could be extended to more general assumptions on the distributions of sample, but, for simplicity, we will make the following sub-Gaussian assumption on the data, conditionally on the past data and the task. Hence, this assumption does not restrict the task distribution in any way.

Assumption 7

(Sub-Gaussian data)
.

There exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}>0
such that, for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\geq 1
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Theta}
, and any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-1)}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{X}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-1}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\sim\density_{#1}(\cdot\nonscript\,|\nonscript\,\mathopen{}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-1)},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})
is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}^{2}
-sub-Gaussian, i.e.,, for any
\lambda>0
,

\log\ex_{#1}\left[e^{\lambda\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}]\rVert^{2}}\right]\leq\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}^{2}\lambda^{2}}{2}\,.

(E.89)

Assumption 8

(Lipschitz model and loss)
.

The models
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
are uniformly Lipschitz in the following sense: there exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}>0
such that,
for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
, any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime}
,

\tfrac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}-1})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}+1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}-1})\rVert\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime}\rVert\,,

(E.90)

The losses
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ell}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}
are uniformly 1-Lipschitz: for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\geq 1
, any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}^{\prime}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{X}}
,

|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ell}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}^{\prime})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ell}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}^{\prime})|\leq\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}^{\prime}\rVert\,.

(E.91)

We will consider the following assumption on the function class
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
.

Assumption 9
.

Assume that the hypothesis class
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
is bounded for w.r.t. some distance
\dist
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
and that, the following extended Lipschitz condition holds: for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}^{\prime}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
, any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}
, any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\geq 1
, any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime}
,
for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
, any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}-1})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}+1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}-1})-\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}^{\prime}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}-1})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}^{\prime}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}+1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}-1})\right)\rVert

(E.92)

\displaystyle\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}^{\prime}\rVert\dist({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}^{\prime})\,.

(E.93)

Note that
8
is implied of
9
when the constant function equal to zero is in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\dist({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}},0)
.

We denote by
\lVert X\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}
the
L^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}
norm of a random variable
X
, i.e.,
\lVert X\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}=(\ex[\lVert X\rVert^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}])^{1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}h}}
.

Lemma E.8
.

For any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}\in[2,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}]
integer, under
5
,
6
,
7
and
8
, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\left\{\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right\}

(E.94)

\displaystyle\quad-\ex\left[\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\left\{\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right\}\right]\Bigg\|_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}

(E.95)

\displaystyle\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}

(E.96)

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}^{3/2}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1-1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}

(E.97)

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]}\right\rVert_{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1-1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\,,

Proof.

and

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}g}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N})})

(E.135)

\displaystyle g({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r})

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}^{3/2}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1-1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1-1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}r}}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\,.

(E.136)

Applying
Lemma

E.5
then gives the desired concentration bound.
∎

\displaystyle\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\Bigg({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}

then gives that

is bounded by the

\displaystyle\ex\left[\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})}\right]

which concludes the proof.
∎

E.5
Generalization bounds for ICL

Putting together the concentration bound from
Proposition

E.1
and the complexity bound from
Lemma

E.9
, we obtain the following generalization bound for ICL:

Theorem E.2

(Generalization bound for ICL)
.

Under
5
,
6
,
7
,
8
and
9
, for any
\delta\in(0,e^{-2}]
, for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}\in(0,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}e^{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}]
,
with probability at least
1-\delta
, the generalization gap

\displaystyle\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})}

(E.154)

is bounded by

(a)

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}\geq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}e^{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}
,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\sqrt{(\log({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta})+1)}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\right)

(E.155)

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\sqrt{(\log({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta})+1)}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{2}

(E.156)

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\left((\log({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta})+1)^{3/2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}^{3/2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\right)\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}

(E.157)

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\sqrt{(\log({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta})+1)}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]}\right\rVert_{2}

(E.158)

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\left((\log({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta})+1){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\right)\frac{1}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}

(E.159)

(b)

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}<{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}e^{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}
,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}^{1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})\right)\bigg({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}

(E.160)

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}^{3/2}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1-1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}}\sqrt{\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}B}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\right)^{2}}\left\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}

(E.161)

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]}\right\rVert_{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}^{1-1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}}\left(\sum_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)\left\lVert{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}-\ex[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{1}]}\right\rVert_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}\bigg),,

(E.162)

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}>0
is a universal constant and where the Dudley-type integral
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}_{\dist}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}})
is defined as

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{I}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q})=\int_{0}^{\Delta}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{N}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}},\dist,u)\right)^{1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}}du\,,\quad\text{ with }\Delta=\diam_{\dist}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}})=\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}^{\prime}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\dist({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}^{\prime})\,.

(E.163)

Proof.

The result is obtained by combining
Proposition

E.1
and
Lemma

E.9
: we write the decomposition

\displaystyle\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\left\{\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})}\right\}

(E.164)

\displaystyle=\ex\left[\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\left\{\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})}\right\}\right]

(E.165)

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\left\{\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})}\right\}-\ex\left[\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{F}}}\left\{\ex\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})\right]-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widehat{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}L}}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}f},({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}})_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}n}\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N}})}\right\}\right]\,,

(E.166)

and we bound
Eq.

E.165
using
Lemma

E.9
and
Eq.

E.166
with high probability using
Proposition

E.1
.

∎

Appendix F
Additional details on examples

F.1
Example: Volterra equation model

We discuss the Volterra equation model to explicit the dependence of the generalization bounds on the memory decay parameter
\alpha>0
.

Setup.

Let
(W_{t})_{t\geq 1}
be noise sequence taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
Given a Lipschitz drift
b\,,mathbbR^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
with Lipschitz constant
L\geq 0
,
we consider the discretized Volterra equation: for
t\geq 0
,

X_{t+1}\;=\;\sum_{u=1}^{t}K(t,u)\,\bigl(b(X_{u})+W_{u}\bigr),\qquad K(t,u)\;=\;\frac{1}{(t-u+1)^{\alpha}},\ \ \alpha>0.

(F.1)

When applying the generalization framework, we would consider the augmented sequence
(X_{1},W_{1},X_{2},W_{2},\ldots)
.
To satisfy the weak dependence assumption
5
, we need to bound the effect of perturbations in either the state or the noise or the drift.
We begin with perturbations in the state or noise, and we discuss drift perturbations at the end of this section.
For perturbations in the state or noise, we will obtain bounds on the Wasserstein distance between the conditional laws of
X_{t}
and
X^{\prime}_{t}
given the past, where
X_{t}
and
X^{\prime}_{t}
are two versions of the process (
F.1
) that differ by a perturbation at some time
s<t
.

The coefficient
\alpha
will play a key role in the dependence structure through the sums:

H_{\alpha}(n)\;=\;\sum_{r=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r^{\alpha}}.

(F.2)

We also use
\zeta(\alpha)=\sum_{r=1}^{\infty}r^{-\alpha}
for
\alpha>1
and we have the following bounds on
H_{\alpha}(n)

H_{\alpha}(n)\;\leq\;\begin{cases}1+\log n,&\alpha=1,\\[4.0pt]
\zeta(\alpha),&\alpha>1.\end{cases}

(F.3)

We will make use of the following technical lemma.

Lemma F.1
.

Let
(a_{n})_{n\geq 0}
be nonnegative numbers and suppose that for
n\geq 1
,

a_{n}\;\leq\;L\sum_{r=1}^{n}r^{-\alpha}\,a_{n-r}\;+\;g_{n},

(F.4)

with non-decreasing
(g_{n})_{n\geq 1}
and given
a_{0}\geq 0
.
Define, for
N\geq 1
,

\lambda_{N}\coloneqq\begin{cases}L(1+\log N)&\text{ if }\alpha=1,\\[6.0pt]
L\zeta(\alpha)&\text{ if }\alpha>1.\end{cases}

(F.5)

Then, for all
1\leq n\leq N
,

a_{n}\;\leq\;\lambda_{N}^{n}a_{0}\;+\;\sum_{j=1}^{n}g_{j}{\lambda_{N}^{n-j}}.

(F.6)

Proof.

Let
A_{n}:=\max_{0\leq m\leq n}a_{m}
. From (
F.4
),

a_{n}\leq L\sum_{r=1}^{n}r^{-\alpha}A_{n-r}+g_{n}\leq LH_{\alpha}(n)A_{n-1}+g_{n}
, so
A_{n}\leq LH_{\alpha}(n)A_{n-1}+g_{n}
since
(g_{n})_{n}
is non-decreasing.
Bounding
H_{\alpha}(n)
using (
F.3
) gives
A_{n}\leq\lambda_{N}A_{n-1}+g_{n}
for all
1\leq n\leq N
.
Iterating this inequality yields the result.
∎

State perturbation.

Fix
s\geq 1
and let
\mathcal{F}_{s}:=\sigma\big(X_{1},\dots,X_{s},\ W_{1},\dots,W_{s}\big)
on which we condition.
Assume the two systems agree up to
s-1
, and at time
s
we have

X^{\prime}_{s}\;=\;X_{s}-h

with
h\neq 0
.
For
t\geq s
, define
\Delta_{t}:=X_{t}-X^{\prime}_{t}
.
Subtracting (
F.1
) for the two evolutions (they share
(W_{u})
) gives for
t\geq s
:

\Delta_{t+1}=\sum_{u=s}^{t}\frac{b(X_{u})-b(X^{\prime}_{u})}{(t-u+1)^{\alpha}},\qquad\|\Delta_{t+1}\|\leq L\sum_{u=s}^{t}\frac{\|\Delta_{u}\|}{(t-u+1)^{\alpha}}.

(F.7)

Set
n:=t-s+1
,
a_{n}:=\mathbb{E}\big(\|\Delta_{s+n}\|\,\big|\,\mathcal{F}_{s}\big)
and
a_{0}=\|\Delta_{s}\|=\|h\|
.
Applying Lemma
F.1
with
g_{n}=0
yields, for
n\leq N
,

a_{n}\;\leq\;\lambda_{N}^{n}\,\|h\|,

(F.8)

We now bound the Wasserstein distance between the conditional laws of
X_{s+n}
and
X^{\prime}_{s+n}
given
\mathcal{F}_{s}
by using the synchronous coupling between
X_{s+n}
and
X^{\prime}_{s+n}
(which share the same noise sequence
(W_{u})_{u>s}
):

W_{1}\!\big(\mathcal{L}(X_{s+n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{s}),\ \mathcal{L}(X^{\prime}_{s+n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{s})\big)\leq\mathbb{E}\big(\|X_{s+n}-X^{\prime}_{s+n}\|\mid\mathcal{F}_{s}\big)\leq\lambda_{N}^{n}\,\|h\|.

Therefore, for any horizon
T\geq s+1
,

{\quad\sup_{\,s+1\leq t\leq T}W_{1}\!\big(\mathcal{L}(X_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{s}),\ \mathcal{L}(X^{\prime}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{s})\big)\;\leq\;\|h\|\,\lambda_{T-s}^{T-s}=\begin{cases}\|h\|\,(L(1+\log(T-s))^{T-s}&\text{if }\alpha=1,\\[6.0pt]
\|h\|\,(L\zeta(\alpha))^{T-s}&\text{if }\alpha>1.\end{cases}\quad}

(F.9)

The behaviour of the bound crucially depends on
\alpha
and
L
: if
\alpha>1
and
L\zeta(\alpha)<1
, the effect of the perturbation decays exponentially fast with
T-s
; if
\alpha>1
and
L\zeta(\alpha)>1
, the effect of the perturbation grows exponentially fast with
T-s
.
In both case, higher values of
\alpha
(faster memory decay) lead to better dependence properties.

Noise perturbation.

Fix
s\geq 1
and let
\mathcal{F}_{s-1}:=\sigma\big(X_{1},\dots,X_{s-1},\ W_{1},\dots,W_{s-1}\big)
.
Assume the two systems agree up to time
s
except that at time
s
we have

W^{\prime}_{s}\;=\;W_{s}+\eta

with
\eta\neq 0
,
and
W^{\prime}_{u}=W_{u}
for
u\neq s
.
Again define
\Delta_{t}:=X_{t}-X^{\prime}_{t}
for
t\geq s
.
Subtracting the two recursions gives for
t\geq s
:

\Delta_{t+1}=\sum_{u=s}^{t}\frac{b(X_{u})-b(X^{\prime}_{u})}{(t-u+1)^{\alpha}}\;+\;\frac{W_{s}-W^{\prime}_{s}}{(t-s+1)^{\alpha}}.

(F.10)

Taking norms and using Lipschitzness,

\|\Delta_{t+1}\|\;\leq\;L\sum_{u=s}^{t}\frac{\|\Delta_{u}\|}{(t-u+1)^{\alpha}}\;+\;\frac{\|\eta\|}{(t-s+1)^{\alpha}}.

Set
n:=t-s+1
and
a_{n}:=\mathbb{E}\big(\|\Delta_{s+n}\|\,\big|\,\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\big)
.
Note
a_{0}=0
(since
X_{s}=X^{\prime}_{s}
).
Apply Lemma
F.1
with
g_{n}:=\|\eta\|\,n^{-\alpha}
to obtain,for
n\leq N
,

a_{n}\;\leq\;\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\eta\|j^{-\alpha}\,{\lambda_{N}^{n-j}}\leq\|\eta\|\times\frac{\lambda_{N}^{n}-1}{\lambda_{N}-1}\,,

(F.11)

where we consider
\lambda_{N}\neq 1
for simplicity.

Bounding the Wasserstein distance as before yields, for any horizon
T\geq s+1
,

{\quad\sup_{\,s+1\leq t\leq T}W_{1}\!\big(\mathcal{L}(X_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{s-1}),\ \mathcal{L}(X^{\prime}_{t}\mid\mathcal{F}_{s-1})\big)\;\leq\;\begin{cases}\|\eta\|\,\frac{(L(1+\log(T-s)))^{T-s}-1}{L(1+\log(T-s))-1},&\text{if }\alpha=1,\\[6.0pt]
\|\eta\|\,\frac{(L\zeta(\alpha))^{T-s}-1}{L\zeta(\alpha)-1},&\text{if }\alpha>1.\end{cases}\quad}

(F.12)

Drift perturbation.

To consider drift perturbations, we write the drift as
b_{\theta}
where
\theta
is a parameter. In addition to assuming that
b_{\theta}
is uniformly
L
-Lipschitz for all
\theta
, we also assume that it is
M
-Lipschitz in
\theta
uniformly in
x
, that is, for all
x,x^{\prime}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{R}}}^{d}
and
\theta,\theta^{\prime}
,

\lVert b_{\theta}(x)-b_{\theta^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\rVert\ \leq\ L\,\|x-x^{\prime}\|+M\,\|\theta-\theta^{\prime}\|.

(F.13)

Consider
\theta,\theta^{\prime}
and the two systems with drifts
b_{\theta}
and
b_{\theta^{\prime}}
respectively:

\displaystyle X_{t+1}\;

\displaystyle=\;\sum_{u=1}^{t}K(t,u)\,\bigl(b_{\theta}(X_{u})+W_{u}\bigr),

(F.14)

\displaystyle X^{\prime}_{t+1}\;

\displaystyle=\;\sum_{u=1}^{t}K(t,u)\,\bigl(b_{\theta^{\prime}}(X^{\prime}_{u})+W_{u}\bigr).

(F.15)

As before, we will bound the Wasserstein distance between
X_{t}
and
X^{\prime}_{t}
by using the synchronous coupling. Assuming that the two sequences share the same noise sequence
(W_{u})
, we define
\Delta_{t}=X_{t}-X^{\prime}_{t}
and obtain, using (
F.13
), for
t\leq T

\|\Delta_{t+1}\|\leq L\sum_{u=1}^{t}\frac{\|\Delta_{u}\|}{(t-u+1)^{\alpha}}+M\|\theta-\theta^{\prime}\|H_{\alpha}(T)\,.

(F.16)

Setting
a_{n}=\lVert\Delta_{n}\rVert
and
g_{n}=M\|\theta-\theta^{\prime}\|H_{\alpha}(T)
with
a_{0}=0
, we can apply Lemma
F.1
as before to obtain, for
t\leq T
,

W_{1}\!\big(\mathcal{L}(X_{t}),\ \mathcal{L}(X^{\prime}_{t})\big)\;\leq\;M\|\theta-\theta^{\prime}\|\begin{cases}(1+\log T)\,\frac{(L(1+\log T))^{t}-1}{L(1+\log T)-1},&\text{if }\alpha=1,\\[6.0pt]
\zeta(\alpha)\,\frac{(L\zeta(\alpha))^{t}-1}{L\zeta(\alpha)-1},&\text{if }\alpha>1\end{cases}

(F.17)

where we used (
F.3
) to bound
H_{\alpha}(T)
.

F.2
Examples for task selection assumptions

In this section, we check that the examples of
Section

3.1
in the main text satisfy
3
and
4
.
These are lengthy but mostly straightforward calculations, which we sketch to illustrate how to verify the assumptions in practice.

Example F.1

(Linear regression)
.

We consider the linear regression example of
Section

3.1
in the main text and check that it satisfies
3
and
4
.

Fix a true task
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\ast}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{R}}}^{d}
. For
t=1,\dots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}
, consider

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}^{2}I_{d})
and noise
\epsilon_{t}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2})
i.i.d., and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}^{\top}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\ast}+\epsilon_{t}
,
z_{t}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t})
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}=\{z_{t}\}_{t=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}
.
Define
Q\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{R}}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\times d}
has rows
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}^{\top}
and
Y=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t})_{t=1}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}
,
and, for any parameter
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{R}}}^{d}
,

\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}):=\log\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})=-\tfrac{1}{2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\|Y-Q{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|_{2}^{2}+\text{const},

where the constant term depends on
Q
but not on
\theta

Let us begin with the tail behavior.
Both
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}^{T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\ast}+\epsilon_{t}
are sub-Gaussian; hence for some
c>0
and all
R\geq 1
,

\prob\!\left(\exists\,t\leq n\,,\|z_{t}\|\geq R\right)\ \leq\ \poly(n)\,e^{-cR^{2}}\ \leq\ \poly(n)\,e^{-R}.

For the tail condition on the likelihood, let
\Delta={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}
and
r_{0}:=Y-Q{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}
. Then

\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})-\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})=-\tfrac{1}{2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\big(\|Q\Delta\|_{2}^{2}-2\Delta^{\top}Q^{\top}r_{0}\big)

Now, by e.g.,
Wainwright 2019
, for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}
large enough, there is
c>0
constant such that,
with probability at least
1-e^{-c{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}
,

\|Q\Delta\|\geq c\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\,\|\Delta\|
and
\|Q^{\top}r_{0}\|\leq c^{-1}\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}\,\|r_{0}\|
.
Hence, uniformly over
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|\geq R
(so
\|\Delta\|\geq R-\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|
),

\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})-\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})\ \leq\ -\tfrac{c^{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}{2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\|\Delta\|^{2}+\tfrac{c^{-1}\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\|\Delta\|\,\|r_{0}\|.

For all
R
larger than a constant multiple of
\|r_{0}\|/\sqrt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}+\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|
, the right-hand side is negative; thus

\sup_{\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|\geq R}\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})<\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})
.
Since
\|r_{0}\|
is sub-Gaussian and the norm bounds above hold with probability at least
1-e^{-cn}\geq 1-e^{-c{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}R}}
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}R}\geq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}
, we obtain, for all
R\geq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}
,

\prob\!\left(\sup_{\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|\geq R}\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})\ \geq\ \density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})\right)\ \leq\ \poly({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T})\,e^{-R}.

We now consider the moment condition.
Then, for any reference
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}
,

\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})}=\exp\!\Big(\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}\{\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})-\ell_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}T}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})\}\Big)\leq\exp\!\Big(\tfrac{1}{2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\,\|Y-Q{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|_{2}^{2}\Big),

Therefore, we have

\log^{2}\!\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})}\ \leq\ C\,\big(\|Q({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\ast}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})\|_{2}^{2}+\|\epsilon\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{2},

and using Gaussian moment bounds

\mathbb{E}\!\left[\log^{2}\!\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})}\right]\ \leq\ \poly(n)\big(1+\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\ast}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|_{2}^{4}\big)\ =\ \poly(n).

We finally check the local regularity condition.
For any
t
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}
,

\log\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{1:t},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{1:t-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{1:t},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{1:t-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime})}=-\tfrac{1}{2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\!\big[({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\top}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t})^{2}-({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}-{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}}^{\top}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t})^{2}\big].

Assuming that
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{1:t}\|_{\infty},|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{1:t}|\leq R
and
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}\|\leq R
(with
R\geq 1
) and using that
(a-b)^{2}-(a-c)^{2}=(c-b)(2a-b-c)
, we have

\Big|\log\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{1:t},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{1:t-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{1:t},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{1:t-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime})}\Big|=\tfrac{1}{2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\,|({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime})^{\top}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}|\,\big|2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}y}_{t}-({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime})^{\top}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}q}_{t}\big|\ \leq\ \tfrac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\,R^{3}\,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}\|,

so the condition holds.

Example F.2

(Ornstein–Uhlenbeck process)
.

We consider the Ornstein–Uhlenbeck (OU) process example of
Section

3.1
in the main text and check that it satisfies
3
and
4
.
For simplicity, we consider the one-dimensional case
d=1
; the extension to
d>1
with diagonal diffusion is straightforward.
We consider tasks
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}=(\mu,\tau)
where
\mu\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{R}}}
and
\tau\in[\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau}]
with
0<\overline{\tau}\leq\underline{\tau}<\infty
. Given
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}
, the Ornstein–Uhlenbeck (OU) SDE

\mathrm{d}X_{t}=\tau(\mu-X_{t})\,\mathrm{d}t+\sigma\,\mathrm{d}W_{t}

is observed at regular times
t_{r}=r\,\Delta_{t}
(
r=1,\dots,n
). We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}:=X_{t_{r}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}=\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}\}_{r=1}^{n}
. The Markov transition is Gaussian with mean

m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}(x):=\mu+e^{-\tau\Delta_{t}}(x-\mu)=e^{-\tau\Delta_{t}}x+\bigl(1-e^{-\tau\Delta_{t}}\bigr)\mu

and variance
v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}:=\mathrm{Var}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})=\sigma^{2}\,\frac{1-e^{-2\tau\Delta_{t}}}{2\tau}\,.
For any path
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:n}
, define
\ell_{n}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}):=\log\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}).

Recall
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}=(\mu,\tau)
with
\tau\in[\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau}]
, discretization step
\Delta_{t}
, and

m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}(x)=\mu+\rho_{\tau}(x-\mu)=\rho_{\tau}x+(1-\rho_{\tau})\mu,\qquad v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}=\sigma^{2}\frac{1-\rho_{\tau}^{2}}{2\tau},\qquad\rho_{\tau}:=e^{-\tau\Delta_{t}}.

Fix a reference
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}=(\mu_{0},\tau_{0})
, write
m_{0}:=m_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}}
,
v_{0}:=v_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}}
, and let
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}=({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{n})
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}
the OU samples at times
r\Delta_{t}
.
The one–step densities are Gaussian, hence

\log\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})}=\sum_{r=1}^{n}\Bigg\{-\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}{v_{0}}-\frac{\big({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})\big)^{2}}{2v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}+\frac{\big({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})\big)^{2}}{2v_{0}}\Bigg\}.

(F.18)

Let us begin with the tail behavior.
Each one-step innovation
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})
is Gaussian with variance
v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}
and

0<v_{\min}\ \leq\ v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\ \leq\ v_{\max}<\infty,\quad v_{\min}:=\sigma^{2}\frac{1-e^{-2\underline{\tau}\Delta_{t}}}{2\underline{\tau}},\;v_{\max}:=\sigma^{2}\frac{1-e^{-2\overline{\tau}\Delta_{t}}}{2\overline{\tau}}.

Moreover, if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1}
satisfies
|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1}|\leq R
, then
m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})
also satisfies
|m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})|\leq\rho_{\underline{\tau}}R+(1-\rho_{\underline{\tau}})|\mu|
.
Hence, there exists
c>0
depending only on
(\Delta_{t},\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\sigma)
and the law of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{0}
such that, for all
R\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\prob\!\Big(\exists r\leq n\,,|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}|\geq R\Big)

(F.19)

\displaystyle\leq\ \prob\!\Big(\exists r\leq n\,,|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})|\geq(1-\rho_{\underline{\tau}})R-|\mu|\Big)

(F.20)

\displaystyle\leq\ \poly(n)\,e^{-cR^{2}}\ \leq\ \poly(n)\,e^{-R},

(F.21)

for
R
large enough compared to
|\mu|
.

Let us continue with the tail condition on the likelihood.
We have the bound

\Big|\sum_{r=1}^{n}\!-\tfrac{1}{2}\log\tfrac{v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}{v_{0}}\Big|\leq\tfrac{n}{2}\,\log\!\frac{v_{\max}}{v_{\min}}=:C_{\mathrm{var}}\,n.

(F.22)

For each
r
, abbreviate
m:=m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})
and
m_{0}:=m_{0}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})
. Using
v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\geq v_{\min}
and
v_{0}\geq v_{\min}
,

-\frac{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m)^{2}}{2v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}+\frac{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0})^{2}}{2v_{0}}\leq\frac{1}{2v_{\min}}\Big(({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0})^{2}-({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m)^{2}\Big).

Expanding the square,

({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0})^{2}-({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m)^{2}=-\big(m-m_{0}\big)^{2}+2\,({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0})\,\big(m-m_{0}\big).

Summing over
r
and applying Cauchy–Schwarz,

\sum_{r=1}^{n}\!\left(-\frac{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m)^{2}}{2v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}+\frac{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0})^{2}}{2v_{0}}\right)\leq-\frac{1}{2v_{\min}}\sum_{r=1}^{n}\Delta_{r}^{2}\;+\;\frac{1}{v_{\min}}\Big(\sum_{r=1}^{n}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0})^{2}\Big)^{\!1/2}\Big(\sum_{r=1}^{n}\Delta_{r}^{2}\Big)^{\!1/2},

(F.23)

where
\delta_{r}:=m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})-m_{0}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})
.

On events where
|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:n}|\leq R
, we have the
conditions

c\lVert\mu-\mu_{0}\rVert-C(1+R)|\delta_{r}|\leq L(1+R)\,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|,

for constants
c,C,L
depending only on
(\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\Delta_{t})
. Therefore, for
\lVert\mu-\mu_{0}\rVert
larger than a constant multiple of
(1+R)
, we have

\sum_{r=1}^{n}\delta_{r}^{2}\;\geq\;n\,c\,\|\mu-\mu_{0}\|^{2}\quad\text{and}\quad\Big(\sum_{r=1}^{n}\delta_{r}^{2}\Big)^{\!1/2}\;\leq\;\sqrt{n}\,C(1+R)\,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|,

(F.24)

for constants
c,C
depending only on
(\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\Delta_{t})
.

Combining (
F.22
), (
F.23
), and (
F.24
),

\log\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})}\;\leq\;Cn-{cn}\|\mu-\mu_{0}\|^{2}+\,\Big(\sum_{r=1}^{n}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1}))^{2}\Big)^{\!1/2}{\sqrt{n}C(1+R)}\,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}}\|,,

(F.25)

for constants
c,C
depending only on
(\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\Delta_{t})
.

Fix
R\geq 1
and assume that
|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:n}|\leq R
: we have shown that it holds with probability at least
1-\poly(n)e^{-cR^{2}}
.

In that case,

\Big(\sum_{r=1}^{n}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{0}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1}))^{2}\Big)^{\!1/2}
in

Eq.

F.25

is bounded
\bigoh\left(\sqrt{n}R\right)
so the RHS can be made negative for all sufficiently large
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|
: more precisely, it is negative for
\lVert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\rVert\geq R^{\prime}
with
R^{\prime}\geq C(1+R)^{2}
for a constant
C
depending only on
(\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\Delta_{t})
.
Since the event we are considering holds with probability at least
1-\poly(n)e^{-cR^{2}}
, it means that it holds with probability at least
1-\poly(n)e^{-R^{\prime}}
.
This proves the required tail bound with
R\leftarrow R^{\prime}
.

Moving to the moment condition, by Gaussian moment bounds,
Eq.

F.18
readily implies

\mathbb{E}\!\left[\log^{2}\!\sup_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}_{0}})}\right]\;\leq\;C\,n^{2}\;=\;\poly(n),

which verifies the likelihood-ratio moment condition in
3
.

Finally, we show the local regularity condition.
For fixed
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:r-1}
, the conditional density is

\log\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:r-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})=-\tfrac{1}{2}\log(2\pi v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})-\frac{\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}-m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})\bigr)^{2}}{2v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}}.

On sets where
|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:r}|\leq R
,
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|\leq R
(so
\mu,\tau
bounded) and with
\tau\in[\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau}]
, the maps

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\mapsto m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})=e^{-\tau\Delta_{t}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1}+\bigl(1-e^{-\tau\Delta_{t}}\bigr)\mu,\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\mapsto v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}=\sigma^{2}\frac{1-e^{-2\tau\Delta_{t}}}{2\tau}

are smooth with bounded first derivatives:

|\partial_{\mu}m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}|\leq 1,\ |\partial_{\tau}m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}|\leq C_{R},\ |\partial_{\tau}v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}|\leq C,\ \partial_{\mu}v_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}=0.

Since
x_{r}-m_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r-1})
is also bounded by a constant multiple of
R
on these sets, we obtain, for all
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}
with
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}\|\leq R
,

\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:r}|\leq R\\
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}\|,\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}\|\leq R\end{subarray}}\left|\log\frac{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:r-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta})}{\density_{#1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{r}\mid{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}_{1:r-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime})}\right|\ \leq\poly(R)\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\theta}^{\prime}\|\,.
